# Natives available in the Lua plugin (LUA.asi)

> **Source:** `LUA.asi` (file version 1.0.0.1, 3,523,584 bytes, modified 2015-05-20, SHA-256 `5e2df81067670945a7838a6681aa61e60c2ea449a15600a40bc3bf9375de2065`)  
> **Method:** the printable strings in the binary were extracted by `ModDevelopment/tools/ApiDocGen`. The plugin's tolua bindings store each namespace name followed by its function names, so the namespace grouping and the names are exact. Parameters and argument handling are **not** in the strings: for those, see the linked NativeDB entry and test in game.  
> **Current names:** matched to NativeDB by name, old name or hash (see [natives/README.md](natives/README.md) for that source).

Lua Plugin by Headscript (2015). Call them as `NAMESPACE.NAME(args)`, for example `PLAYER.PLAYER_PED_ID()`.

LUA.asi was built in 2015, so it uses the **old namespace and function names**. The "Current name" column gives today's NativeDB name, which you need when you look a native up. Names starting with `_0x` had no name in 2015. A native added to the game after 2015 can't be called from this plugin.

Lua namespace to current namespace: `GAMEPLAY` is `MISC`, `UI` is `HUD`, `CONTROLS` is `PAD`, `AI` is `TASK`, `TIME` is `CLOCK`, `WORLDPROBE` is `SHAPETEST`, `CAM` is `CAMERA`, `PATHFIND` is `PATH`, `APP` is `APPS`, `ITEMSET` is `ITEMSETS`, `NETWORKCASH` is `MONEY`, `SYSTEM` is `BUILTIN`. `UNK`, `UNK1` to `UNK3`, `UNK_SC`, `DLC1` and `DLC2` were split up.

4824 natives in 41 namespaces: [PLAYER](#player) (204), [ENTITY](#entity) (157), [PED](#ped) (528), [VEHICLE](#vehicle) (493), [OBJECT](#object) (103), [AI](#ai) (275), [GAMEPLAY](#gameplay) (269), [AUDIO](#audio) (219), [CUTSCENE](#cutscene) (43), [INTERIOR](#interior) (34), [CAM](#cam) (210), [WEAPON](#weapon) (86), [ITEMSET](#itemset) (9), [STREAMING](#streaming) (114), [SCRIPT](#script) (25), [UI](#ui) (418), [GRAPHICS](#graphics) (302), [STATS](#stats) (147), [BRAIN](#brain) (11), [MOBILE](#mobile) (24), [APP](#app) (17), [TIME](#time) (16), [PATHFIND](#pathfind) (52), [CONTROLS](#controls) (44), [DATAFILE](#datafile) (50), [FIRE](#fire) (29), [ZONE](#zone) (9), [ROPE](#rope) (42), [WATER](#water) (8), [WORLDPROBE](#worldprobe) (10), [NETWORK](#network) (648), [NETWORKCASH](#networkcash) (76), [DLC1](#dlc1) (33), [DLC2](#dlc2) (9), [SYSTEM](#system) (23), [DECORATOR](#decorator) (10), [UNK_SC](#unk_sc) (45), [UNK](#unk) (8), [UNK1](#unk1) (9), [UNK2](#unk2) (3), [UNK3](#unk3) (12)
